1. Population App
  • Introduction
  • Balkans
  • Caucasus
  • Population App

Population App

Population app is an R Shiny app where you can select multiple countries in the Country field and view their populations. You can also adjust both handles of the Year range slider to compare population data across custom timeframes.

#| '!! shinylive warning !!': |
#|   shinylive does not work in self-contained HTML documents.
#|   Please set `embed-resources: false` in your metadata.
#| standalone: true
#| viewerHeight: 800

library(shiny)
library(dplyr)
library(readr)
library(echarts4r)
library(bslib)
library(vroom)

df <- read_csv("data.csv")

ui <- page_sidebar(
  theme = bslib::bs_theme(bootswatch = "cosmo"),
  title = "Population App",
  sidebar = sidebar(
    selectInput("country", "Country", choices = unique(df$country),
                multiple = TRUE, selected = c("Greece", "Bulgaria", "Albania")),
    sliderInput("year", "Year", min = 1950, max = 2026,
                value = c(1950, 2026))
  ),
  card(
    card_header("Population graph"),
    echarts4rOutput("plot")
  )
)

server <- function(input, output, session) {
  data <- reactive({
    req(input$country)

    df |> filter(
        country %in% input$country,
        year >= input$year[1] & year <= input$year[2]
      )

  })

  output$plot <- renderEcharts4r({
    validate(
      need(input$country, "Select at least one country"),
      need(diff(input$year) > 0, "Ending year should be greater than starting year")
    )
    
    data() |> 
      group_by(country) |> 
      e_charts(year) |> 
      e_tooltip(trigger = "axis", backgroundColor = "#FFFFFF") |>
      e_line(population, symbol = "none") |> 
      e_x_axis(name = "Year", min = input$year[1], max = input$year[2]) |> 
      e_y_axis(name = "Population")|> 
      # e_theme("azul") |>
      e_animation(show = FALSE)
  })
}

shinyApp(ui = ui, server = server)
